        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "The world of TFC is full of animal life. Some animals are here to help, and some are incredibly dangerous. This section is about wild animals. For information on livestock, animals that can give you items you need, see the Animal Husbandry page.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "Animals can be grouped into a few different categories: Predators, Prey, and Aquatic animals. The next few pages will detail each of these categories of animals.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "Predators are animals that can attack the player. They are either Nocturnal, only hunting at night, or Diurnal, only hunting during the day. Predators can be neutral or hostile, depending on if they have killed a target recently. Predators have a home territory that they will defend - if you run far enough away, the predator will stop chasing and return home.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "Polar Bear",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.name",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "The polar bear spawns in only the coldest regions, temperature at most 10°C, with a rainfall of at least 100mm.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "Grizzly Bear",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.name",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "The grizzly bear spawns in forests of moderate climates, with a temperature range of -15 to 15°C and at least 200mm of rainfall.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "Black Bear",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.name",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "The black bear spawns in forests of warmer, wetter climates, of temperature 5 to 20°C and at least 250mm of rainfall.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "The cougar prefers most moderate climates, with temperature from -10 to 21°C and at least 150mm of rainfall.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "The panther prefers most moderate climates, with temperature from -10 to 21°C and at least 150mm of rainfall.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.7.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "The lion spawns in plains with an average temperature of at least 16°C, and rainfall between 50 and 300mm.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.8.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "The tiger spawns in forests with an average temperature of at least 13°C, and rainfall above 100mm.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.9.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "The sabertooth spawns at any temperature above 0°C, and any rainfall above 250mm.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.10.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "The wolf spawns at any temperature below 22°C, and rainfall between 70 and 480mm. They hunt in packs. Feeding a wolf enough times will tame it into a Dog.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.11.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "The direwolf spawns at any temperature below freezing, and rainfall between 150 and 420mm. They hunt in packs. They are larger than regular wolves, and are not tamable.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.12.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "The hyena spawns at any temperature warmer than 15°C, and rainfall between 80 and 380mm. They hunt in packs. They are not tamable.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.13.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "The crocodile spawns near rivers, lakes, and marshes at any temperature warmer than 15°C, and at any rainfall. They are nocturnal, and most dangerous in the water.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.14.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "The ocelot attacks small animals. It spawns at any temperature from 15 to 30°C, and rainfall between 300 and 500mm, in forests. Feeding an ocelot enough times will tame it into a Cat. Ocelots can eat raw fish",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.15.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "Ramming animals occasionally attempt to charge nearby creatures, including the player. Their attacks are powerful, but can be dodged. They will ram more frequently if attacked.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.16.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "The boar spawns at temperature below 25°C and above -5°C, and rainfall between 130 and 400mm.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.17.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "The moose spawns in forests at temperature below 10°C and above -15°C, and rainfall between 150 and 300mm.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.18.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "The wildebeest spawns in open plains at any temperature above 13°C, and rainfall between 90 and 380mm.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.19.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "The bison spawns in open plains at temperature below 15°C and above -10°C, and rainfall between 160 and 420mm.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.20.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "Prey animals fear players and predators. They are adept at fleeing from danger, but generally cannot fight back. Some prey animals enjoy snacking on crops.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.22.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "The rabbit is known to chew on carrots and cabbage. They are ubiquitous in the world, changing their coat based on climate. They only need 15mm of rainfall to spawn.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.23.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "The fox likes to eat the berries off of bushes. It can be found in forests with temperature below 25°C, and rainfall between 130 and 400mm.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.24.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "The deer spawns in forests at any temperature below 25°C and above -15°C, and rainfall between 130 and 400mm.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.25.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "The caribou spawns at any temperature below -9°C, and rainfall between 110 and 500mm.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.26.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "The gazelle spawns in open plains at any temperature above 12°C, and rainfall between 90 and 380mm.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.27.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "The bongo spawns in forests at any temperature above 15°C, and rainfall between 230 and 500mm.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.28.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "The grouse spawns at any temperature below 13°C and above -12°C, and rainfall between 150 and 400mm.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.29.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "The pheasant spawns in forests at any temperature below 17°C and above -5°C, and rainfall between 100 and 300mm.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.30.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "The turkey spawns in forests at any temperature below 17°C and above 0°C, and rainfall between 250 and 450mm.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.31.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "The peafowl spawns in forests at any temperature above 14°C, and rainfall between 190 and 500mm.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.32.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "The lemming spawns at any temperature below -3°C and above -12°C, and rainfall between 160 and 500mm.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.33.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "The jerboa spawns at any temperature below 25°C and above -3°C, and rainfall below 160mm.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.34.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "The mongoose spawns at any temperature above 12°C, and rainfall between 160 and 500mm.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.35.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "Aquatic Animals are a broad category which covers a number of different behaviors. They may be Shore Dwellers, Fish, Shellfish, or Large Aquatic Creatures",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.36.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "Aquatic Animals",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.36.title",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "Shore Animals only spawn on sea shores and spend some of their day swimming, and some walking on the beach. They are curious creatures, and will follow the player around, but cannot be tamed.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.37.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "The penguin spawns in only the coldest beaches, with temperature of at most -14°C and rainfall of at least 75mm.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.38.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "Sea Turtle",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.39.name",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "The sea turtle likes warm water. It spawns in temperature of at least 21°C and rainfall of at least 250mm.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.39.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "Fish are small creatures that swim in water. Most of them can be fished. Some prefer oceans, rivers, or lakes.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.40.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "Cod prefer colder oceans, temperature at most 18°C. They can be fished.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.41.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "Pufferfish live in any ocean with at least a temperature of 10°C.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.42.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "Jellyfish live in warmer oceans, with a temperature of at least 18°C.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.43.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "Tropical Fish",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.44.name",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "Tropical fish prefer warmer oceans, with a temperature of at least 18°C.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.44.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "Salmon spawn in any river or lake with a temperature of at least -5°C.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.45.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "Bluegill spawn in any river or lake with a temperature of at least -10°C and at most 26°C.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.46.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "Shellfish are small animals that live on the floor of bodies of water. They cannot be fished, but drop shells that can be eaten or made into flux. Shellfish can be bait for fish.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.48.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "Isopods spawn in deeper sections of oceans of temperature at most 14°C.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.49.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "Lobster spawn in any ocean that is at most of a temperature of 21°C.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.50.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "Crayfish are like lobster, but spawn in rivers and lakes. They need a temperature of at least 5°C and a rainfall of at least 125mm.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.51.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "Horseshoe Crab",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.52.name",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "Horseshoe crabs spawn in oceans of moderate climate, temperature between 10 and 21°C and with a rainfall of at most 400mm.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.52.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "Large Water Creatures are larger animals that live in the bigger bodies of water. Some of them hunt fish. They drop blubber, which can be made into lamp fuel.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.54.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "Large Water Creatures",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.54.title",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "Orca whales live in deep oceans with temperature of at most 19°C and rainfall of at least 100mm.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.55.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "Dolphins live in deep oceans with temperature of at least 10°C and rainfall of at least 200mm.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.56.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_animals.json",
          "text": "Manatees live in any warm lake, with temperature of at least 20°C and rainfall of at most 300mm.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.57.text",
          "label": "Wild Animals",
          "item_ref_count": 32,
          "count": 60
        }
      ],

      "semantic_evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Ores and Minerals",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "The ores you can find Beneath are mostly similar to what you can find on the surface, but with a few changes. Consult the Ores in TFG category for exact details.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "The most common rock type found is Migmatite. This deep metamorphic rock contains a wide variety of ores you'd normally find in other rock types on the surface.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "In wetter areas, you may find Travertine. It is a sedimentary rock that can contain the same kind of ores as Migmatite.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Around the deepest level of the Beneath, the different rock types gradually all turn into Pyroxenite. This ultramafic igneous rock is extremely rich in ores, but this depth also comes with extreme heat, so be prepared!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Anthracite is a powerful fuel. It can be found in Migmatite in the upper half of the Beneath.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Ores and Minerals",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Beneath the overworld, you will still find ore veins to exploit. It is important to note that Netherrack does not collapse, like raw stone would. However, Blackstone, Nether Bricks, Cobblerack and miscellaneous other blocks do. Prospecting will still work Beneath the overworld.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Nether Gold",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.name",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Nether Gold replaces netherrack, and is common. Beneath lava, there is more gold, and richer.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Cursecoal is a powerful fuel. It replaces netherrack above level 80.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Sylvite replaces basalt at any level.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Nether Quartz",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.name",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Nether Quartz replaces netherrack at all levels, and is common.",

        {

        {

          "text": "Wild Fruits",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Wild Fruits",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 56
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_fruits",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_fruits.json",
          "text": "Many different varieties of wild fruits can be found growing in the world. These can be collected to be eaten, or farmed, with the right equipment. These can be found on different varieties of bushes or trees. In general, fruits can be found in three types of plants: Fruit Trees, Tall Bushes, and Small Bushes. All fruiting plants have a common lifecycle. They will grow, form flowers, sprout fruit, and then lay dormant in a yearly cycle.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Wild Fruits",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 56
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_fruits",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_fruits.json",
          "text": "Fruit plants are seasonal. During their cold season, these plants will appear brown and lifeless. In the spring, they become green and healthy, getting ready to produce fruit and grow larger. The exact times this happen varies by the fruit. Fruit plants can die, as well: of old age, and of improper climate conditions.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Wild Fruits",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 56
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_fruits",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_fruits.json",
          "text": "Fruit trees grow from tiny saplings into large, flowering trees. The branches of fruit trees are their heart, and they will grow as long as the climate conditions are right. As fruit trees mature, they will grow leaves all around their branches. The leaves can flower and fruit depending on the season.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Wild Fruits",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 56
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",

          "text": "Fruit Trees",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.title",
          "label": "Wild Fruits",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 56
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_fruits",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_fruits.json",
          "text": "A typical fruit tree.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Wild Fruits",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 56
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_fruits",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_fruits.json",
          "text": "Fruit trees start out at Saplings. Saplings will only start growing, placing their first piece of the tree, if it is not the dormant season for that fruit. The size of the finished tree is loosely determined by how many saplings are in the original sapling block. More saplings means a bigger tree. More saplings can be added to a single block through Splicing. To splice a sapling into another, just on it while holding a sapling and a Knife in your off hand.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Wild Fruits",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 56
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_fruits",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_fruits.json",
          "text": "To get saplings from a fruit tree, break the 'elbow' blocks (branch blocks that are attached to a block on one side and above) tree with an Axe. Saplings can also be placed on these 'elbow' sections, if they are not too high up in the tree. This allows one fruit tree to grow multiple fruits. Harvesting fruit is done with when the leaf block is bearing fruit. This will give one fruit, and revert the plant back to its growing stage, until it goes dormant for the winter.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Wild Fruits",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 56
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_fruits",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_fruits.json",
          "text": "Temperature: 5 - 25 °C Rainfall: 100 - 350mm Cherry trees grow in the months of January through March, start flowering in April and May, and bear fruit in June.",

          "label": "Wild Fruits",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 56
        },

        {

          "text": "Temperature: 1 - 25 °C Rainfall: 100 - 280mm Red Apple trees grow from March to July, start flowering in August and September, and bear fruit in October.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.20.text",

        {

        },
        {

          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_fruits.json",

        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_fruits",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_fruits.json",
          "text": "Temperature: 17 - 35 °C Rainfall: 280 - 500mm Bananas are a special kind of fruit tree. They grow only vertically, lack leaves, and only fruit at the topmost block. Saplings are dropped from the flowering part of the plant. Once a banana plant is harvested, it dies, and will not produce any more fruit. It must be replanted in the spring.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.22.text",
          "label": "Wild Fruits",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 56
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_fruits",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_fruits.json",
          "text": "Banana Tree",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.22.title",
          "label": "Wild Fruits",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 56
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_fruits",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_fruits.json",
          "text": "An example banana tree.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.23.text",
          "label": "Wild Fruits",

          "count": 56
        },
        {

          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_fruits",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_fruits.json",
          "text": "The fruiting calendar for fruit trees.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.24.text",
          "label": "Wild Fruits",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 56
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_fruits",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_fruits.json",
          "text": "Fruit Trees",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.24.title",
          "label": "Wild Fruits",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 56
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_fruits",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_fruits.json",
          "text": "Tall Bushes are fruit blocks that are able to grow in all directions, and spread. They do this by either growing directly upwards, up to three high, or placing canes on their sides, which can mature into full bush blocks. After a while, the bushes will stop spreading, and reach maturity. Harvesting these bushes with a sharp tool has a chance to drop a new bush. Bushes that are fully mature will always drop themselves.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.26.text",
          "label": "Wild Fruits",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 56
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_fruits",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_fruits.json",
          "text": "Tall Bushes",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.26.title",
          "label": "Wild Fruits",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 56
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_fruits",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_fruits.json",
          "text": "A wild tall bush.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.27.text",
          "label": "Wild Fruits",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 56
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_fruits",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_fruits.json",
          "text": "Tall bushes are able to spread when their canes have somewhere to take root. Practically, this means that they need a solid block under them to place a new bush on. Providing a flat, open area free of grass or other debris gives them the best chance to grow.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.28.text",
          "label": "Wild Fruits",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 56
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_fruits",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_fruits.json",
          "text": "Bushes, unlike fruit trees, take into account surrounding water blocks to determine their Hydration, unlike fruit trees, which only care about rainfall. Any full bush block can grow berries, which are harvestable with .",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.29.text",
          "label": "Wild Fruits",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 56
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_fruits",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_fruits.json",
          "text": "Temperature: 7 - 24 °C Rainfall: 24 - 100mm Blackberry bushes grow from February to May, start flowering in June and July, and bear fruit in August. They can be found in areas with few trees.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.30.text",
          "label": "Wild Fruits",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 56
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_fruits",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_fruits.json",
          "text": "Blackberry Bush",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.30.title",
          "label": "Wild Fruits",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 56
        },
        {

          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_fruits.json",
          "text": "An example blackberry bush.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.31.text",
          "label": "Wild Fruits",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 56
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_fruits",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_fruits.json",
